```
     1→'use client';
     2→
     3→import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
     4→import Link from 'next/link';
     5→import { BookTocItem, BookChapterEntry, Heading } from '@/lib/markdown';
     6→import { useLanguage } from './LanguageProvider';
     7→import { useSidebarAutoScroll } from '@/hooks/useSidebarAutoScroll';
     8→import InlineBookToc from './InlineBookToc';
     9→
    10→interface BookSidebarProps {
    11→  bookSlug: string;
    12→  bookTitle: string;
    13→  toc: BookTocItem[];
    14→  chapters: BookChapterEntry[];
    15→  currentChapter: string;
    16→  headings?: Heading[];
    17→}
    18→
    19→export default function BookSidebar({ bookSlug, bookTitle, toc, chapters, currentChapter, headings = [] }: BookSidebarProps) {
    20→  const { t } = useLanguage();
    21→  const currentIndex = chapters.findIndex(ch => ch.id === currentChapter);
    22→  const currentItemRef = useRef<HTMLLIElement>(null);
    23→  const sidebarRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
    24→
    25→  // Track which parts are collapsed
    26→  const [collapsedParts, setCollapsedParts] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>(() => {
    27→    const initial: Record<string, boolean> = {};
    28→    for (const item of toc) {
    29→      if ('part' in item) {
    30→        const containsCurrent = item.chapters.some(ch => ch.id === currentChapter);
    31→        initial[item.part] = !containsCurrent;
    32→      }
    33→    }
    34→    return initial;
    35→  });
    36→
    37→  const togglePart = (part: string) => {
    38→    setCollapsedParts(prev => ({ ...prev, [part]: !prev[part] }));
    39→  };
    40→
    41→  useSidebarAutoScroll(sidebarRef, currentItemRef, currentChapter);
    42→
    43→  // Expand part containing current chapter when it changes
    44→  useEffect(() => {
    45→    for (const item of toc) {
    46→      if ('part' in item && item.chapters.some(ch => ch.id === currentChapter)) {
    47→        setCollapsedParts(prev => ({ ...prev, [item.part]: false }));
    48→      }
    49→    }
    50→  }, [currentChapter, toc]);
    51→
    52→
    53→  // Pre-calculate chapter global indices to avoid reassignment during render
    54→  const chapterIndices = new Map<string, number>();
    55→  let currentGlobalIdx = 0;
    56→  toc.forEach((item) => {
    57→    if ('part' in item) {
    58→      item.chapters.forEach(ch => {
    59→        chapterIndices.set(ch.id, currentGlobalIdx++);
    60→      });
    61→    } else {
    62→      chapterIndices.set(item.id, currentGlobalIdx++);
    63→    }
    64→  });
    65→
    66→  // Helper to render a chapter link + inline headings if current
    67→  const renderChapterItem = (ch: { title: string; id: string }) => {
    68→    const isCurrent = ch.id === currentChapter;
    69→    const idx = chapterIndices.get(ch.id) ?? 0;
    70→    const isPast = idx < currentIndex;
    71→
    72→    return (
    73→      <li key={ch.id} ref={isCurrent ? currentItemRef : undefined}>
    74→        <Link
    75→          href={`/books/${bookSlug}/${ch.id}`}
    76→          className={`block py-2 px-3 rounded-lg text-sm no-underline transition-all duration-200 ${
    77→            isCurrent
    78→              ? 'bg-accent/10 text-accent font-semibold border-l-2 border-accent'
    79→              : isPast
    80→                ? 'text-foreground/70 hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted/5'
    81→                : 'text-muted hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted/5'
    82→          }`}
    83→          aria-current={isCurrent ? 'page' : undefined}
    84→        >
    85→          {ch.title}
    86→        </Link>
    87→        {isCurrent && <InlineBookToc headings={headings} />}
    88→      </li>
    89→    );
    90→  };
    91→
    92→  return (
    93→    <aside
    94→      ref={sidebarRef}
    95→      className="hidden lg:block sticky top-20 self-start w-[280px] max-h-[calc(100vh-6rem)] overflow-y-auto pr-4 scrollbar-hide hover:scrollbar-thin"
    96→    >
    97→      {/* Book Header */}
    98→      <div className="mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-muted/10">
    99→        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
   100→          <span className="text-[10px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-accent">
   101→            {t('book')}
   102→          </span>
   103→          <span className="text-xs font-mono text-muted whitespace-nowrap">
   104→            {currentIndex + 1}/{chapters.length}
   105→          </span>
   106→        </div>
   107→        <Link href={`/books/${bookSlug}`} className="group block no-underline">
   108→          <h3 className="font-serif font-bold text-heading text-lg leading-snug group-hover:text-accent transition-colors">
   109→            {bookTitle}
   110→          </h3>
   111→        </Link>
   112→      </div>
   113→
   114→      {/* TOC */}
   115→      <nav aria-label="Book navigation">
   116→        <ul className="space-y-1">
   117→          {toc.map((item, tocIdx) => {
   118→            if ('part' in item) {
   119→              const isCollapsed = collapsedParts[item.part];
   120→              const partChapters = item.chapters;
   121→
   122→              return (
   123→                <li key={`part-${tocIdx}`}>
   124→                  <button
   125→                    onClick={() => togglePart(item.part)}
   126→                    className="w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-2 py-2 px-2 -mx-2 rounded-lg text-left hover:bg-muted/5 transition-colors group"
   127→                  >
   128→                    <span className="text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted group-hover:text-foreground transition-colors">
   129→                      {item.part}
   130→                    </span>
   131→                    <svg
   132→                      className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 text-muted flex-shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200 ${isCollapsed ? '' : 'rotate-180'}`}
   133→                      fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}
   134→                    >
   135→                      <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7" />
   136→                    </svg>
   137→                  </button>
   138→
   139→                  {!isCollapsed && (
   140→                    <ul className="space-y-0.5 mt-1 mb-3 animate-slide-down">
   141→                      {partChapters.map((ch) => renderChapterItem(ch))}
   142→                    </ul>
   143→                  )}
   144→                </li>
   145→              );
   146→            } else {
   147→              return renderChapterItem(item);
   148→            }
   149→          })}
   150→        </ul>
   151→      </nav>
   152→
   153→      {/* Footer */}
   154→      <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-muted/10">
   155→        <Link
   156→          href={`/books/${bookSlug}`}
   157→          className="text-xs font-sans text-muted hover:text-accent transition-colors no-underline flex items-center gap-1"
   158→        >
   159→          {t('all_books')}
   160→          <svg className="w-3 h-3" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}>
   161→            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M9 5l7 7-7 7" />
   162→          </svg>
   163→        </Link>
   164→      </div>
   165→    </aside>
   166→  );
   167→}
```

```
    32→          <svg
    33→            className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 transition-transform duration-200 ${collapsed ? '' : 'rotate-180'}`}
    34→            fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={2}
    35→          >
    36→            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7" />
    37→          </svg>
    38→        </button>
    39→      </div>
    40→      {!collapsed && (
    41→        <ul className="space-y-0.5 border-l border-muted/15 animate-slide-down">
    42→          {headings.map(h => {
    43→            const isActive = h.id === activeHeadingId;
    44→            return (
    45→              <li key={h.id}>
    46→                <a
    47→                  href={`#${h.id}`}
    48→                  onClick={e => scrollToHeading(e, h.id)}
    49→                  className={`block py-1 text-[13px] leading-snug no-underline transition-colors duration-200 ${
    50→                    h.level === 3 ? 'pl-6' : 'pl-3'
    51→                  } ${
    52→                    isActive
    53→                      ? 'text-accent font-medium border-l-2 border-accent -ml-px'
    54→                      : 'text-foreground/70 hover:text-foreground'
    55→                  }`}
    56→                >
    57→                  {h.text}
    58→                </a>
    59→              </li>
    60→            );
    61→          })}
    62→        </ul>
    63→      )}
    64→    </nav>
    65→  );
    66→}
```

```
     1→import type React from 'react';
     2→
     3→export function scrollToHeading(e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>, id: string): void {
     4→  e.preventDefault();
     5→  const element = document.getElementById(id);
     6→  if (element) {
     7→    const elementPosition = element.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY;
     8→    window.scrollTo({ top: elementPosition - 80, behavior: 'smooth' });
     9→    history.pushState(null, '', `#${id}`);
    10→  }
    11→}
```

| # | Issue | Fix? | Reasoning |

| 5 `BookLayout.tsx:70` | URL helpers | **Yes** | Trivial now that `getBookChapterUrl()` exists |
| 6 `BookMobileNav.tsx:55` | URL helpers | **Yes** | Same — consistent with #5 |
| 7 `BookSidebar.tsx:41` | Auto-scroll bug | **Yes** | Real UX bug — sidebar won't scroll to current chapter if it's in a collapsed part |
| 8 `TocPanel.tsx:31` | ARIA i18n | **Yes** | Legitimate gap — component already uses `t()` but ARIA labels are hardcoded English |
| 9 `scroll-utils.ts:6` | `preventDefault` order | **Yes** | Correctness bug — native anchor fallback broken if element not found |

```
     1→'use client';
     2→
     3→import { useEffect, type RefObject } from 'react';
     4→
     5→export function useSidebarAutoScroll(
     6→  sidebarRef: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>,
     7→  itemRef: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>,
     8→  dep: unknown,
     9→): void {
    10→  useEffect(() => {
    11→    if (itemRef.current && sidebarRef.current) {
    12→      const item = itemRef.current;
    13→      const sidebar = sidebarRef.current;
    14→      sidebar.scrollTop = item.offsetTop - sidebar.clientHeight / 2 + item.offsetHeight / 2;
    15→    }
    16→  // refs are stable; only re-run when dep changes
    17→  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    18→  }, [dep]);
    19→}
    20→
```
